Company brief

GLENCORE NIKKELVERK AS is a private limited company operating in Produksjon av ikke-jernholdige metaller ellers. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 2.1bn, annual result NOK -875.1m. Annual accounts available for 2011-2025 (15 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs. Revenue growing (+5.2% YoY).
